=== Question 1 ===
A healthcare company is developing an application to process medical queries. The application must answer complex queries with high accuracy by reducing semantic dilution. The application must refer to domain-specific terminology in medical documents to reduce ambiguity in medical terminology. The application must be able to respond to 1,000 queries each minute with response times less than 2 seconds.
Which solution will meet these requirements with the LEAST operational overhead?

A. Create an Amazon Bedrock agent to orchestrate multiple AWS Lambda functions to decompose queries. Create an Amazon Bedrock knowledge base to store the reference medical documents. Use the agent's built-in knowledge base capabilities. Add deep research and reasoning capabilities to the agent to reduce ambiguity in the medical terminology.
B. Use Amazon SageMaker Al to host custom ML models for both query decomposition and query expansion. Configure Amazon Bedrock knowledge bases to store the reference medical documents. Encrypt the documents in the knowledge base.
C. Configure an Amazon Bedrock knowledge base to store the reference medical documents. Enable query decomposition in the knowledge base. Configure an Amazon Bedrock flow that uses a foundation model (FM) and the knowledge base to support the application. (CORRECT)
D. Use Amazon API Gateway to route incoming queries to an Amazon Bedrock agent. Configure the agent to use an Anthropic Claude model to decompose queries and an Amazon Titan model to expand queries. Create an Amazon Bedrock knowledge base to store the reference medical documents.

Correct answer(s): C

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
Truy vấn
phức tạp, độ chính xác cao (complex queries, high accuracy)
Giảm pha loãng ngữ nghĩa (reduce semantic dilution)
+ thuật ngữ chuyên ngành y tế
1000 queries/phút, <2s,
ít vận hành nhất (LEAST operational overhead)
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Configure an Amazon Bedrock knowledge base to store the reference medical documents. Enable query decomposition in the knowledge base. Configure an Amazon Bedrock flow that uses a foundation model (FM) and the knowledge base to support the application.
Query decomposition là tính năng built-in
của Bedrock Knowledge Bases, chuyên giải quyết "complex queries" và "semantic dilution" bằng cách tách câu hỏi phức tạp thành sub-queries.
Bedrock Flows
điều phối low-code → ít vận hành nhất.
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Use Amazon API Gateway to route incoming queries to an Amazon Bedrock agent. Configure the agent to use an Anthropic Claude model to decompose queries and an Amazon Titan model to expand queries. Create an Amazon Bedrock knowledge base to store the reference medical documents.
→ Sai. Tự dựng agent + 2 model riêng để decompose/expand → nhiều vận hành hơn hẳn so với dùng tính năng có sẵn.
❌ Use Amazon SageMaker Al to host custom ML models for both query decomposition and query expansion. Configure Amazon Bedrock knowledge bases to store the reference medical documents. Encrypt the documents in the knowledge base.
→ Sai. Self-host custom ML model = vận hành cực cao, loại ngay khi gặp "LEAST overhead".
❌ Create an Amazon Bedrock agent to orchestrate multiple AWS Lambda functions to decompose queries. Create an Amazon Bedrock knowledge base to store the reference medical documents. Use the agent's built-in knowledge base capabilities. Add deep research and reasoning capabilities to the agent to reduce ambiguity in the medical terminology.
→ Sai. Agent + nhiều Lambda để decompose = thừa, "deep research" mơ hồ, overhead cao.
🔑
Tips and tricks:
complex queries + semantic dilution → Query Decomposition (built-in KB feature)
LEAST overhead + orchestration → Bedrock Flows
(không tự code agent/Lambda)

============================================================

=== Question 2 ===
A GenAI developer is evaluating Amazon Bedrock foundation models (FMs) to enhance a Europe-based company's internal business application. The company has a multi-account landing zone in AWS Control Tower. The company uses SCPs to allow its accounts to use only the eu-north-1 Region and the eu-west-1 Region. All customer data must remain in private networks within the approved AWS Regions.
The GenAI developer selects an FM based on analysis and testing and hosts the model in the eu-central-1 Region and the eu-west-3 Region. The GenAI developer must enable access to the FM for the company’s employees. The GenAI developer must ensure that requests to the FM are private and remain with the same Regions as the FM.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Deploy the FM on Amazon EC2 instances in eu-north-1. Deploy a private Amazon API Gateway REST API in front of the EC2 instances. Configure an Amazon Bedrock VPC endpoint. Integrate the REST API with the business application.
B. Deploy the FM in Amazon SageMaker AI in eu-north-1. Configure a SageMaker AI VPC endpoint. Extend existing SCPs to allow employees to use the SageMaker AI endpoint. Integrate the FM in SageMaker AI with the business application.
C. Deploy an AWS Lambda function that is exposed by a private Amazon API Gateway REST API to a VPC in eu-north-1. Create a VPC endpoint for the selected FM in eu-central-1 and eu-west-3. Extend existing SCPs to allow employees to use the FM. Integrate the REST API with the business application. (CORRECT)
D. Configure the FM to use cross-Region inference through an eu.amazon.* endpoint to ensure that all calls remain within Europe. Configure an Amazon Bedrock VPC endpoint. Extend existing SCPs to allow employees to use the FM through inference profiles in Europe-based Regions where the FM is available. Use an inference profile to integrate Amazon Bedrock with the business application.

Correct answer(s): C

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
Công ty ở châu Âu đang đánh giá các Foundation Model (FM) trên Amazon Bedrock cho ứng dụng nội bộ.
Công ty sử dụng môi trường multi-account với AWS Control Tower.
SCP chỉ cho phép sử dụng 2 Region:
eu-north-1
eu-west-1
Toàn bộ dữ liệu khách hàng phải:
Không đi qua Internet công cộng.
Luôn nằm trong các AWS Region đã được phê duyệt.
GenAI Developer chọn một FM trên Bedrock.
Model này được host tại:
eu-central-1
eu-west-3
Yêu cầu:
Cho phép nhân viên truy cập Foundation Model.
Request tới FM phải đi qua mạng riêng (private network).
Dữ liệu không được ra Internet.
Dữ liệu phải ở cùng Region với nơi model được host (eu-central-1 hoặc eu-west-3).
Vẫn phải tuân thủ các giới hạn Region của tổ chức.
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Deploy an AWS Lambda function that is exposed by a private Amazon API Gateway REST API to a VPC in eu-north-1. Create a VPC endpoint for the selected FM in eu-central-1 and eu-west-3. Extend existing SCPs to allow employees to use the FM. Integrate the REST API with the business application.
Private API Gateway + VPC endpoint
đảm bảo traffic
private
(PrivateLink).
VPC endpoint đặt
đúng tại eu-central-1/eu-west-3
= request ở lại đúng Region của FM.
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Deploy the FM on Amazon EC2 instances in eu-north-1. Deploy a private Amazon API Gateway REST API in front of the EC2 instances. Configure an Amazon Bedrock VPC endpoint. Integrate the REST API with the business application.
→ Sai. Bedrock FM
không deploy lên EC2
→ sai bản chất.
❌ Configure the FM to use cross-Region inference through an eu.amazon.* endpoint to ensure that all calls remain within Europe. Configure an Amazon Bedrock VPC endpoint. Extend existing SCPs to allow employees to use the FM through inference profiles in Europe-based Regions where the FM is available. Use an inference profile to integrate Amazon Bedrock with the business application.
→ Sai.
Cross-Region inference
sẽ route sang Region khác → vi phạm "remain with the SAME Regions as the FM" (chỉ "within Europe" là chưa đủ chặt).
❌ Deploy the FM in Amazon SageMaker AI in eu-north-1. Configure a SageMaker AI VPC endpoint. Extend existing SCPs to allow employees to use the SageMaker AI endpoint. Integrate the FM in SageMaker AI with the business application.
→ Sai. Đây là Bedrock FM, không phải SageMaker model → sai dịch vụ.
🔑
Tips and tricks:
private access tới Bedrock → VPC endpoint (PrivateLink)
"remain within SAME Region as FM" → KHÔNG dùng cross-Region inference

=== Question 3 ===
A large ecommerce company has deployed a foundation model (FM) to generate product descriptions. The company's engineering team monitors technical metrics such as token usage, latency, and error rates by using Amazon CloudWatch. The company's marketing team tracks business metrics such as conversion rates and revenue impact in its own systems.
The company needs a unified observability solution that correlates technical performance with business outcomes. The solution must provide automatic alerts to stakeholders when operational metrics indicate degradation. The solution must provide comprehensive visibility across both technical and business metrics.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Create CloudWatch dashboards that include technical metrics and imported business metrics. Configure CloudWatch composite alarms that combine technical data and business data. Use Amazon SNS to set up notifications to stakeholders.
B. Use Amazon Managed Grafana to visualize technical metrics from CloudWatch with business metrics from external sources. Configure Amazon Managed Grafana alerts to invoke AWS Lambda functions. Configure the Lambda functions to remediate issues automatically when metrics exceed predefined thresholds.
C. Stream CloudWatch metrics to Amazon S3 by using CloudWatch metric streams. Create Amazon QuickSight dashboards to visualize the combined technical metrics and business metrics. Set up Amazon EventBridge rules to send notifications to stakeholders when metrics exceed predefined thresholds.
D. Configure CloudWatch custom dashboards that integrate operational metrics with imported business metrics. Set up CloudWatch composite alarms with anomaly detection. Use Amazon SNS to create alarm actions to notify stakeholders when correlated metrics indicate performance issues. (CORRECT)

Correct answer(s): D

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
Giải pháp quan sát thống nhất (unified observability solution)
cho ecommerce dùng FM
Chỉ số kỹ thuật (technical metrics)
: token usage, latency, error rates → đang trên
CloudWatch
Chỉ số kinh doanh (business metrics)
: conversion rates, revenue → trên
hệ thống riêng
Yêu cầu:
tự động cảnh báo (automatic alerts)
,
tương quan (correlate)
cả 2 loại metrics
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Configure CloudWatch custom dashboards that integrate operational metrics with imported business metrics. Set up CloudWatch composite alarms with anomaly detection. Use Amazon SNS to create alarm actions to notify stakeholders when correlated metrics indicate performance issues.
Composite alarms
cho phép kết hợp nhiều alarm (cả technical lẫn business metrics) thành 1 alarm duy nhất → đáp ứng yêu cầu "correlate"
Anomaly detection
tự động phát hiện degradation mà không cần đặt ngưỡng thủ công
SNS
xử lý notification đến stakeholders đúng yêu cầu
Toàn bộ nằm trong CloudWatch ecosystem → ít operational overhead nhất
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Create CloudWatch dashboards that include technical metrics and imported business metrics. Configure CloudWatch composite alarms that combine technical data and business data. Use Amazon SNS to set up notifications to stakeholders.
→ Sai. Gần đúng nhưng
thiếu anomaly detection
→ phải set threshold thủ công, kém linh hoạt
❌ Use Amazon Managed Grafana to visualize technical metrics from CloudWatch with business metrics from external sources. Configure Amazon Managed Grafana alerts to invoke AWS Lambda functions. Configure the Lambda functions to remediate issues automatically when metrics exceed predefined thresholds.
→ Sai.
Auto-remediate
không được yêu cầu, chỉ cần alert. Grafana phức tạp hơn cần thiết, operational overhead cao hơn
❌ Stream CloudWatch metrics to Amazon S3 by using CloudWatch metric streams. Create Amazon QuickSight dashboards to visualize the combined technical metrics and business metrics. Set up Amazon EventBridge rules to send notifications to stakeholders when metrics exceed predefined thresholds.
→ Sai. Pipeline quá phức tạp (CloudWatch → S3 → QuickSight). EventBridge không phải công cụ tối ưu cho alarm notifications so với SNS
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Correlate technical + business metrics
→ CloudWatch
Composite Alarms
Automatic alerts on degradation
→ CloudWatch
Anomaly Detection
Notify stakeholders
→
Amazon SNS
(không phải EventBridge)
Unified observability
→ ưu tiên giải pháp
all-in-CloudWatch
thay vì multi-service pipeline

=== Question 4 ===
A medical device company wants to feed reports of medical procedures that used the company's devices into an AI assistant. To protect patient privacy, the AI assistant must expose patient personally identifiable information (PII) only to surgeons. The AI assistant must redact PII for engineers. The AI assistant must reference only medical reports that are less than 3 years old.
The company stores reports in an Amazon S3 bucket as soon as each report is published. The company has already set up an Amazon Bedrock knowledge base. The AI assistant uses Amazon Cognito to authenticate users.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Set up an S3 Lifecycle configuration on the bucket to remove reports that are older than 3 years. Schedule an AWS Lambda function to run daily syncs between the bucket and the knowledge base. When users interact with the AI assistant, call the ApplyGuardrail configuration that matches the user's Cognito user group to redact PII from the agent’s responses if appropriate.
B. Enable Amazon Macie PII detection on the S3 bucket. Use an S3 trigger to invoke an AWS Lambda function that redacts PII from the reports. Configure the Lambda function to delete outdated documents from the bucket and to invoke knowledge base syncing.
C. Invoke an AWS Lambda function to sync the S3 bucket and the knowledge base when a new report is uploaded to the bucket. Use a second Lambda function to invoke Amazon Comprehend to detect and redact PII if a user is part of the engineer Cognito user group. Set up an S3 Lifecycle configuration to remove reports that are older than 3 years from the bucket. (CORRECT)
D. Create a second knowledge base. Set up an S3 Lifecycle configuration on the bucket to remove reports that are older than 3 years. Invoke an AWS Lambda function that syncs the bucket with the original knowledge base when a new report is uploaded to the bucket. Use Amazon Comprehend to detect and redact PII before syncing the bucket with the second knowledge base. When a user interacts with the AI assistant, redirect the model to the appropriate knowledge base depending on the user’s Cognito user group.

Correct answer(s): C

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
AI assistant cho
thiết bị y tế (medical device)
, dùng
Amazon Bedrock Knowledge Base
+
Amazon Cognito
PII
: surgeons được xem, engineers phải
bị redact
Chỉ dùng
báo cáo dưới 3 năm (reports less than 3 years old)
Reports lưu trong
S3
, upload liên tục khi publish
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Invoke an AWS Lambda function to sync the S3 bucket and the knowledge base when a new report is uploaded to the bucket. Use a second Lambda function to invoke Amazon Comprehend to detect and redact PII if a user is part of the engineer Cognito user group. Set up an S3 Lifecycle configuration to remove reports that are older than 3 years from the bucket.
Lambda trigger on S3 upload
→ sync knowledge base
real-time
ngay khi report mới được tạo
Amazon Comprehend
detect & redact PII → phân biệt theo
Cognito user group
(engineer vs surgeon)
S3 Lifecycle
tự động xóa reports > 3 năm → đảm bảo knowledge base chỉ chứa dữ liệu hợp lệ
Kiến trúc event-driven, đơn giản, đúng yêu cầu
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Enable Amazon Macie PII detection on the S3 bucket. Use an S3 trigger to invoke an AWS Lambda function that redacts PII from the reports. Configure the Lambda function to delete outdated documents from the bucket and to invoke knowledge base syncing.
Amazon Macie
thiên về
phát hiện/báo cáo
PII, không phải redact theo user role
Không có cơ chế phân biệt surgeon vs engineer khi serving
❌ Set up an S3 Lifecycle configuration on the bucket to remove reports that are older than 3 years. Schedule an AWS Lambda function to run daily syncs between the bucket and the knowledge base. When users interact with the AI assistant, call the ApplyGuardrail configuration that matches the user's Cognito user group to redact PII from the agent’s responses if appropriate.
Daily sync
→ không real-time, có thể miss reports mới tạo
ApplyGuardrail dùng để filter output của FM, không phải redact PII theo user role đúng cách
❌ Create a second knowledge base. Set up an S3 Lifecycle configuration on the bucket to remove reports that are older than 3 years. Invoke an AWS Lambda function that syncs the bucket with the original knowledge base when a new report is uploaded to the bucket. Use Amazon Comprehend to detect and redact PII before syncing the bucket with the second knowledge base. When a user interacts with the AI assistant, redirect the model to the appropriate knowledge base depending on the user’s Cognito user group.
2 knowledge bases
→ phức tạp, tốn kém, operational overhead cao
Không phân biệt được surgeon vs engineer một cách dynamic
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Redact PII theo role (role-based PII redaction)
→
Amazon Comprehend
+
Cognito user group
Xóa data cũ tự động (auto-expire old data)
→
S3 Lifecycle policy
Sync ngay khi upload (real-time sync)
→
S3 trigger → Lambda
(không phải scheduled)
Macie
→ phát hiện/báo cáo PII, KHÔNG phải redact

=== Question 5 ===
A financial services company wants to use Amazon Bedrock foundation models (FMs) to analyze call center recordings. When calls end, the call center stores recordings as MP3 files in an Amazon S3 bucket. The company needs to generate summaries and sentiment analysis for the recordings in a structured format as soon as new files are created. The recordings average 20 MB in size.
Which combination of solutions will meet these requirements? (Choose two.)

A. Use AWS Step Functions to orchestrate a workflow to process the recordings. Configure steps to invoke Amazon Transcribe to convert audio to text, validate job completion, and to invoke an AWS Lambda function to process the text by using Amazon Bedrock FMs to generate structured analysis output.
B. Use AWS Step Functions to orchestrate a workflow to process the recordings. Configure steps to invoke Amazon Transcribe to convert audio to text, validate job completion, and to directly invoke Amazon Bedrock FMs to generate summaries and sentiment analysis in JSON format. (CORRECT)
C. Configure the source S3 bucket to send notifications to the Step Functions workflow when an object is created in the bucket.
D. Use AWS Step Functions to orchestrate a workflow to process the recordings. Configure steps to invoke Amazon Transcribe to convert audio to text, validate job completion, and to invoke an AWS Lambda function to create a prompt to invoke Amazon Bedrock FMs to generate structured analysis output.
E. Configure the source S3 bucket to send events to Amazon EventBridge. Create an EventBridge rule to invoke the Step Functions workflow when an object is created in the bucket. (CORRECT)

Correct answer(s): B, E

Explanation:
A.
Use AWS Step Functions to orchestrate a workflow to process the recordings. Configure steps to invoke Amazon Transcribe to convert audio to text, validate job completion, and to invoke an AWS Lambda function to process the text by using Amazon Bedrock FMs to generate structured analysis output.

=== Question 6 ===
A university recently digitized a collection of archival documents, academic journals, and manuscripts. The university stores the digital files in an AWS Lake Formation data lake.
The university hires a GenAI developer to build a solution to allow users to search the digital files by using text queries. The solution must return journal abstracts that are semantically similar to a user's query. Users must be able to search the digitized collection based on text and metadata that is associated with the journal abstracts. The metadata of the digitized files does not contain keywords. The solution must match similar abstracts to one another based on the similarity of their text. The data lake contains fewer than 1 million files.
Which solution will meet these requirements with the LEAST operational overhead?

A. Use Amazon Titan Embeddings in Amazon Bedrock to create vector representations of the digitized files. Store embeddings in the OpenSearch Neural Plugin for Amazon OpenSearch Service. (CORRECT)
B. Use Amazon Comprehend to extract topics from the digitized files. Store the topics and file metadata in an Amazon Aurora PostgreSQL database. Query the abstract metadata against the data in the Aurora database.
C. Use Amazon SageMaker AI to deploy a sentence-transformer model. Use the model to create vector representations of the digitized files. Store embeddings in an Amazon Aurora PostgreSQL database that has the pgvector extension.
D. Use Amazon Titan Embeddings in Amazon Bedrock to create vector representations of the digitized files. Store embeddings in an Amazon Aurora PostgreSQL Serverless database that has the pgvector extension.

Correct answer(s): A

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
University số hóa tài liệu, lưu trên
AWS Lake Formation
Cần
semantic search
(tìm kiếm theo nghĩa, không phải keyword)
Search dựa trên
text AND metadata
của journal abstracts
Metadata không chứa keywords
; dữ liệu < 1 triệu files
Yêu cầu:
LEAST operational overhead
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Use Amazon Titan Embeddings in Amazon Bedrock to create vector representations of the digitized files. Store embeddings in the OpenSearch Neural Plugin for Amazon OpenSearch Service.
Amazon Titan Embeddings
là managed embedding model → không cần deploy, maintain model
OpenSearch Neural Plugin
hỗ trợ
k-NN vector search
native → semantic search out-of-the-box
Toàn bộ managed services →
LEAST operational overhead
Hỗ trợ search cả text lẫn metadata embeddings
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Use Amazon Comprehend to extract topics from the digitized files. Store the topics and file metadata in an Amazon Aurora PostgreSQL database. Query the abstract metadata against the data in the Aurora database.
Comprehend topic extraction
≠ semantic similarity search
Aurora PostgreSQL không có native vector search → không phù hợp cho semantic search
❌ Use Amazon SageMaker AI to deploy a sentence-transformer model. Use the model to create vector representations of the digitized files. Store embeddings in an Amazon Aurora PostgreSQL database that has the pgvector extension.
SageMaker deploy & maintain model →
high operational overhead
Mặc dù pgvector hỗ trợ vector search, nhưng vẫn phức tạp hơn OpenSearch Neural Plugin
❌ Use Amazon Titan Embeddings in Amazon Bedrock to create vector representations of the digitized files. Store embeddings in an Amazon Aurora PostgreSQL Serverless database that has the pgvector extension.
pgvector trên Aurora Serverless phức tạp hơn, và
Aurora Serverless
có cold start latency
OpenSearch với Neural Plugin được thiết kế tối ưu hơn cho vector search ở scale < 1M files
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Semantic search + LEAST overhead
→
Amazon Titan Embeddings + OpenSearch Neural Plugin
Metadata không có keywords
→ phải dùng
vector/semantic search
(không phải keyword search)
SageMaker deploy model
→ HIGH operational overhead
OpenSearch Neural Plugin
= giải pháp vector search managed, ít overhead nhất

=== Question 7 ===
A company is using Amazon Bedrock to develop a customer support AI assistant. The AI assistant must respond to customer questions about their accounts. The AI assistant must not expose personal information in responses. The company must comply with data residency policies by ensuring that all processing occurs within the same AWS Region where each customer is located.
The company wants to evaluate how effective the AI assistant is at preventing the exposure of personal information before the company makes the AI assistant available to customers.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Configure a cross-Region Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply a set of content and word filters. Set the guardrail to detect mode during development and testing. Switch to mask mode for production deployment.
B. Configure an Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply content and topic filters. Set the guardrail to detect mode during development, testing, and production. Disable invocation logging for the Amazon Bedrock model.
C. Configure an Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply sensitive information filters. Set the guardrail to mask mode during development and testing. Switch to block mode for production deployment. Deploy a copy of the guardrail to each Region where the company operates. (CORRECT)
D. Configure a cross-Region Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply sensitive information filters. Set the guardrail to detect mode during development and testing. Switch to block mode for production deployment.

Correct answer(s): C

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
AI assistant dùng
Amazon Bedrock
, không được expose
thông tin cá nhân (personal information)
Phải tuân thủ
data residency
→ xử lý trong
cùng AWS Region
với customer
Cần
evaluate
(đánh giá) trước khi deploy → tức đang trong giai đoạn
testing
Yêu cầu: ngăn personal information trong responses
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Configure an Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply sensitive information filters. Set the guardrail to mask mode during development and testing. Switch to block mode for production deployment. Deploy a copy of the guardrail to each Region where the company operates.
Sensitive information filters
đúng loại filter cần dùng (personal info = PII)
Mask mode
khi testing → cho phép evaluate hiệu quả của guardrail mà không block hoàn toàn
Block mode
production → bảo vệ tối đa
Deploy per Region
→ đáp ứng
data residency
requirement (processing within same Region)
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Configure a cross-Region Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply sensitive information filters. Set the guardrail to detect mode during development and testing. Switch to block mode for production deployment.
Cross-Region guardrail
vi phạm
data residency
requirement (data phải xử lý trong cùng Region)
❌ Configure an Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply content and topic filters. Set the guardrail to detect mode during development, testing, and production. Disable invocation logging for the Amazon Bedrock model.
Content/topic filters
≠ sensitive information/PII filters
Dùng
detect mode
trong production → không block được personal information
❌ Configure a cross-Region Amazon Bedrock guardrail to apply a set of content and word filters. Set the guardrail to detect mode during development and testing. Switch to mask mode for production deployment.
Cross-Region
vi phạm data residency
Content/word filters
không phải loại phù hợp cho personal information
Mask
thay vì
block
ở production → kém an toàn hơn
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Personal information / PII
→ Bedrock Guardrails
Sensitive Information Filters
Evaluate trước khi deploy
→
Mask mode
(detect & mask nhưng không block để test)
Data residency + same Region
→
KHÔNG dùng cross-Region guardrail
; deploy
per Region
Block mode
= production |
Mask mode
= testing |
Detect mode
= monitoring only

=== Question 8 ===
A software as a service (SaaS) company is building a recommendation model that uses Amazon SageMaker AI to support an application that recommends airline cabin upgrades to customers. The company will host SageMaker AI models on Amazon Bedrock by using Amazon Bedrock Custom Model Import. Airline companies will use the application to send customized offers to customers.
The model must examine the travel history of customers to help make more relevant recommendations. The company stores customer travel history data in an Amazon RDS database. The company must ensure that the application delivers consistent, relevant, and accurate results across multiple airlines and customer populations.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Implement text-to-SQL transformations with SQL validations to accurately retrieve relevant booking patterns, preferences, and loyalty information from the RDS database. Use the results to generate personalized cabin upgrade recommendations. Apply Amazon Bedrock guardrails to filter responses. Use confidence scoring and semantic similarity searches to reduce hallucinations.
B. Use Amazon OpenSearch Service to implement vector searches of customer travel history embeddings. Use the vector searches to give the application the ability to perform similarity-based retrieval of booking patterns, preferences, and loyalty information to generate personalized cabin upgrade recommendations. Apply Amazon Bedrock guardrails to filter responses. Use confidence scoring and semantic similarity searches to reduce hallucinations.
C. Implement text-to-SQL transformations with SQL validations to accurately retrieve relevant booking patterns, preferences, and loyalty information from the RDS database. Use the results to generate personalized cabin upgrade recommendations. Apply Amazon Bedrock guardrails to filter content. === Question 9 ===

Inappropriate advice solicitation includes requests for unethical practices, harmful activities, or manipulative behaviors. The solution must maintain acceptable overall response times, so all pre-processing filters must finish before the content reaches the FM.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

=== Question 14 ===
A specialty coffee company has a mobile app that generates personalized coffee roast profiles by using Amazon Bedrock with a three-stage prompt chain. The prompt chain converts user inputs into structured metadata, retrieves relevant logs for coffee roasts, and generates a personalized roast recommendation for each customer.
Users in multiple AWS Regions report inconsistent roast recommendations for identical inputs, slow inference during the retrieval step, and unsafe recommendations such as brewing at excessively high temperatures. The company must improve the stability of outputs for repeated inputs. The company must also improve app performance and the safety of the app's outputs. The updated solution must ensure 99.5% output consistency for identical inputs and achieve inference latency of less than 1 second. The solution must also block unsafe or hallucinated recommendations by using validated safety controls.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Deploy Amazon Bedrock with provisioned throughput to stabilize inference latency. Apply Amazon Bedrock guardrails that have semantic denial rules to block unsafe outputs. Use Amazon Bedrock Prompt Management to manage prompts by using approval workflows. (CORRECT)
B. Use Amazon Kendra to improve roast log retrieval accuracy. Store normalized prompt metadata within Amazon DynamoDB. Use AWS Step Functions to orchestrate multistep prompts.
C. Use Amazon Bedrock Agents to manage chaining. Log model inputs and outputs to Amazon CloudWatch Logs. Use logs from Amazon CloudWatch to perform A/B testing for prompt versions.

Coffee app

trên Amazon Bedrock

cho identical inputs,

ở retrieval step,
unsafe recommendations

99.5% output consistency
cho identical inputs
Inference latency < 1 second
Block unsafe/hallucinated recommendations
bằng safety controls
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Deploy Amazon Bedrock with provisioned throughput to stabilize inference latency. Apply Amazon Bedrock guardrails that have semantic denial rules to block unsafe outputs. Use Amazon Bedrock Prompt Management to manage prompts by using approval workflows.
Provisioned throughput
→ đảm bảo consistent performance, giảm latency < 1 second
Semantic denial rules
trong Bedrock Guardrails → block unsafe/hallucinated recommendations theo nghĩa (không chỉ keyword)

→ quản lý prompt versions, đảm bảo
99.5% consistency
cho identical inputs (approved prompts → same output)

❌ Use Amazon Bedrock Agents to manage chaining. Log model inputs and outputs to Amazon CloudWatch Logs. Use logs from Amazon CloudWatch to perform A/B testing for prompt versions.

→ inconsistency (mục tiêu là TĂNG consistency)
Không giải quyết latency và safety requirements
❌ Cache prompt results in Amazon ElastiCache. Use AWS Lambda functions to pre-process metadata and to trace end-to-end latency. Use AWS X-Ray to identify and remediate performance bottlenecks.

consistency
của new/unique inputs

Không có safety controls

Không có safety controls
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Consistent output + stable latency
→
Provisioned Throughput
(không phải on-demand)
Block unsafe/hallucinated outputs
→
Bedrock Guardrails
với
semantic denial rules
Prompt consistency + version control
→
Amazon Bedrock Prompt Management
+ approval workflows
A/B testing prompts
→ TẠO RA inconsistency → sai mục tiêu
Provisioned Throughput
vs
On-demand
: Provisioned = guaranteed capacity, consistent performance

=== Question 15 ===
A retail company is using Amazon Bedrock to develop a customer service AI assistant. Analysis shows that 70% of customer inquiries are simple product questions that a smaller model can effectively handle. However, 30% of inquiries are complex return policy questions that require advanced reasoning. The company wants to implement a cost-effective model selection framework to automatically route customer inquiries to appropriate models based on inquiry complexity. The framework must maintain high customer satisfaction and minimize response latency.
Which solution will meet these requirements with the LEAST implementation effort?

A. Create a multi-stage architecture that uses a small foundation model (FM) to classify the complexity of each inquiry. Route simple inquiries to a smaller, more cost-effective model. Route complex inquiries to a larger, more capable model. Use AWS Lambda functions to handle the routing logic.
B. Use Amazon Bedrock intelligent prompt routing to automatically analyze inquiries. Route simple product inquiries to smaller models, and route complex return policy inquiries to more capable larger models. (CORRECT)
C. Implement a single-model solution that uses an Amazon Bedrock mid-sized foundation model (FM) with on-demand pricing. Include special instructions in model prompts to handle both simple and complex inquiries by using the same model.
D. Create separate Amazon Bedrock endpoints for simple and complex inquiries. Implement a rule-based routing system based on keyword detection. Use on-demand pricing for the smaller model and provisioned throughput for the larger model.

Correct answer(s): B

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
Customer service AI assistant
dùng Amazon Bedrock
70% simple questions
→ smaller model đủ dùng
30% complex questions
(return policy) → cần advanced reasoning model
Cần
auto-route
inquiries đến model phù hợp
Yêu cầu:
high satisfaction
,
minimize latency
,
LEAST implementation effort
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Use Amazon Bedrock intelligent prompt routing to automatically analyze inquiries. Route simple product inquiries to smaller models, and route complex return policy inquiries to more capable larger models.
Intelligent prompt routing
là native Bedrock feature →
LEAST implementation effort
Tự động phân tích độ phức tạp và route đến model phù hợp
Không cần code custom routing logic, không cần maintain multiple endpoints
Tối ưu cost (small model cho simple queries) và latency
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Create a multi-stage architecture that uses a small foundation model (FM) to classify the complexity of each inquiry. Route simple inquiries to a smaller, more cost-effective model. Route complex inquiries to a larger, more capable model. Use AWS Lambda functions to handle the routing logic.
Phải build thêm classification stage + Lambda routing logic →
MORE implementation effort
Thêm latency từ classification step
❌ Implement a single-model solution that uses an Amazon Bedrock mid-sized foundation model (FM) with on-demand pricing. Include special instructions in model prompts to handle both simple and complex inquiries by using the same model.
Không phải cost-effective (không route đến smaller model cho simple queries)
Không "automatically route" theo yêu cầu
Prompt instructions không đảm bảo routing consistency
❌ Create separate Amazon Bedrock endpoints for simple and complex inquiries. Implement a rule-based routing system based on keyword detection. Use on-demand pricing for the smaller model and provisioned throughput for the larger model.
Rule-based keyword detection
→ brittle, không handle edge cases tốt
Maintain 2 endpoints riêng biệt → more operational overhead
Keyword matching kém thông minh hơn intelligent routing
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Auto-route queries đến different models + LEAST effort
→
Amazon Bedrock Intelligent Prompt Routing
70/30 simple/complex routing
→ Bedrock Intelligent Prompt Routing (built-in, không cần code)
Keyword-based routing
→ fragile, NOT recommended cho complex NLP tasks
Multi-stage classification
→ EXTRA latency + effort → không phải LEAST effort
Khi thấy "LEAST implementation effort" + Bedrock + routing → nghĩ ngay đến
native Bedrock features

=== Question 16 ===
A company is designing an API for a generative AI (GenAI) application that uses a foundation model (FM) that is hosted on a managed model service. The API must stream responses to reduce latency, enforce token limits to manage compute resource usage, and implement retry logic to handle model timeouts and partial responses.
Which solution will meet these requirements with the LEAST operational overhead?

A. Integrate an Amazon API Gateway REST API with an AWS Lambda function that invokes Amazon Bedrock. Use Lambda response streaming to stream responses. Enforce token limits within the Lambda function. Implement retry logic by using Lambda and API Gateway timeout configurations.
B. Connect an Amazon API Gateway HTTP API directly to Amazon Bedrock. Simulate streaming by using client-side polling. Enforce token limits on the frontend. Configure retry behavior by using API Gateway integration settings.
C. Connect an Amazon API Gateway WebSocket API to an Amazon ECS service that hosts a containerized inference server. Stream responses by using the WebSocket protocol. Enforce token limits within Amazon ECS. Handle model timeouts by using ECS task lifecycle hooks and restart policies.
D. Integrate an Amazon API Gateway HTTP API with an AWS Lambda function to invoke Amazon Bedrock. Use Lambda response streaming to stream responses. Enforce token limits within the Lambda function. Implement retry logic for model timeouts by using Lambda and API Gateway timeout configurations. (CORRECT)

GenAI API
dùng
foundation model (FM)
trên managed model service

để giảm latency
Enforce token limits
để quản lý compute
Retry logic
cho model timeouts và partial responses
LEAST operational overhead
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Integrate an Amazon API Gateway HTTP API with an AWS Lambda function to invoke Amazon Bedrock. Use Lambda response streaming to stream responses. Enforce token limits within the Lambda function. Implement retry logic for model timeouts by using Lambda and API Gateway timeout configurations.

(không phải REST API) = lighter weight, lower latency, lower cost → LEAST overhead
Lambda response streaming
= native feature cho real-time streaming responses
Token limits enforce trong Lambda = đơn giản, không cần infrastructure thêm
Lambda + API Gateway timeout configurations = retry logic built-in
Các đáp án sai:

Client-side polling ≠ true streaming
→ không đáp ứng yêu cầu stream responses
Enforce token limits "on the frontend" → không reliable
Không có proper retry logic
❌ Connect an Amazon API Gateway WebSocket API to an Amazon ECS service that hosts a containerized inference server. Stream responses by using the WebSocket protocol. Enforce token limits within Amazon ECS. Handle model timeouts by using ECS task lifecycle hooks and restart policies.
ECS container =
HIGH operational overhead
(manage containers, scaling, etc.)
WebSocket phức tạp hơn cần thiết
ECS lifecycle hooks cho retry = phức tạp
❌ Integrate an Amazon API Gateway REST API with an AWS Lambda function that invokes Amazon Bedrock. Use Lambda response streaming to stream responses. Enforce token limits within the Lambda function. Implement retry logic by using Lambda and API Gateway timeout configurations.
REST API
tốn kém hơn và có nhiều overhead hơn
HTTP API
Về mặt kỹ thuật gần giống A nhưng HTTP API (A) là lựa chọn tối ưu hơn cho LEAST overhead
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Streaming responses + LEAST overhead
→
Lambda response streaming
+
HTTP API
(không phải REST API)
HTTP API vs REST API
: HTTP API = cheaper, faster, less features | REST API = more features, more expensive
Client-side polling
≠
true streaming
→ KHÔNG đáp ứng yêu cầu streaming
ECS
→ HIGH operational overhead → loại ngay khi thấy "LEAST overhead"

Which combination of solutions will meet these requirements? (Choose two.)

B. Create an SCP that allows employees to use only approved models. Configure the SCP to require employees to specify a guardrail identifier in calls to invoke an approved model. (CORRECT)
C. Use AWS CloudFormation to create a custom Amazon Bedrock guardrail that has a mask filtering policy. Use stack sets to deploy the guardrail to each account in the organization.

A.
Use AWS CloudFormation to create a custom Amazon Bedrock guardrail that has a block filtering policy. Use stack sets to deploy the guardrail to each account in the organization.

=== Question 22 ===
A company has a generative AI (GenAI) application that uses Amazon Bedrock to provide real-time responses to customer queries. The company has noticed intermittent failures with API calls to foundation models (FMs) during peak traffic periods.
The company needs a solution to handle transient errors and provide detailed observability into FM performance. The solution must prevent cascading failures during throttling events and provide distributed tracing across service boundaries to identify latency contributors. The solution must also enable correlation of performance issues with specific FM characteristics.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Implement a custom retry mechanism with a fixed delay of 1 second between retries. Configure Amazon CloudWatch alarms to monitor the application's error rates and latency metrics.
B. Implement client-side caching of all FM responses. Add custom logging statements in the application code to record API call durations.
C. Configure the AWS SDK with standard retry mode and exponential backoff with jitter. Use AWS X-Ray tracing with annotations to identify and filter service components. (CORRECT)
D. Configure the AWS SDK with adaptive retry mode. Use AWS CloudTrail distributed tracing to monitor throttling events.

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
GenAI app
dùng Bedrock,
real-time responses
,
peak traffic
gây intermittent failures
Cần:
Handle
transient errors
(throttling)
Prevent cascading failures
Distributed tracing
across service boundaries
Correlate performance với
FM characteristics
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Configure the AWS SDK with standard retry mode and exponential backoff with jitter. Use AWS X-Ray tracing with annotations to identify and filter service components.
Exponential backoff with jitter
→ handle throttling, prevent cascading failures (không retry đồng thời)
Standard retry mode
trong AWS SDK → built-in retry với proper backoff
AWS X-Ray
→ distributed tracing across service boundaries
X-Ray annotations
→ correlate traces với specific FM characteristics
Toàn bộ là SDK-level configuration → LEAST overhead
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Implement a custom retry mechanism with a fixed delay of 1 second between retries. Configure Amazon CloudWatch alarms to monitor the application's error rates and latency metrics.
Fixed delay
= không phù hợp, có thể gây thundering herd problem
Custom retry = more overhead vs SDK built-in
CloudWatch alarms ≠ distributed tracing
❌ Implement client-side caching of all FM responses. Add custom logging statements in the application code to record API call durations.
Caching tất cả FM responses không phù hợp cho real-time responses
Custom logging ≠ distributed tracing
Không address retry/cascading failure
❌ Configure the AWS SDK with adaptive retry mode. Use AWS CloudTrail distributed tracing to monitor throttling events.
CloudTrail
= API audit logging, KHÔNG phải distributed tracing tool
Adaptive retry mode
= điều chỉnh retry rate nhưng không có jitter → vẫn có risk cascading
X-Ray (B) tốt hơn CloudTrail cho distributed tracing
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Prevent cascading failures + handle throttling
→
Exponential backoff with jitter
Distributed tracing across services
→
AWS X-Ray
(không phải CloudTrail)
Correlate performance với FM
→
X-Ray annotations
CloudTrail
= API audit |
X-Ray
= distributed tracing → KHÔNG nhầm lẫn!
Fixed delay retry
→ thundering herd problem → SAI

bị reach khi Regional traffic spikes

,
minimize operational complexity
✅

→ tự động route trong geographic boundary (EU data stays in EU)

=== Question 33 ===
Example Corp provides a personalized video generation service that millions of enterprise customers use. - personalized video generation, dùng proprietary GenAI model

"data accessor"

chứa dữ liệu nội bộ của họ (product preferences, business history...) — dữ liệu
vẫn nằm trong tài khoản/quyền kiểm soát của customer

cấp quyền "data accessor"

secure API
tại thời điểm cần enrich prompt.

Ownership & control

Semantic accuracy cao + latency thấp

Tối thiểu kiến trúc phức tạp
: Example Corp
không phải tự deploy hạ tầng

customer
, không phải Example Corp.
Các đáp án sai:

Real-time retrieval
→ đề nói không cần real-time → unnecessary complexity
❌ Ensure that each customer configures an Amazon Bedrock knowledge base. Allow cross-account querying so Example Corp can retrieve structured data for prompt augmentation.
Bedrock Knowledge Base không có cơ chế "data accessor" chuẩn hoá dành riêng cho mô hình multi-tenant B2B như Q Business.
Cross-account querying kiểu này đòi hỏi Example Corp phải tự thiết lập cơ chế truy cập, quản lý permission phức tạp cho
hàng triệu
customer riêng lẻ →
tăng độ phức tạp kiến trúc
, đi ngược lại yêu cầu "minimize architectural complexity".

Việc

đồng nghĩa index (và gián tiếp là dữ liệu) được
chia sẻ trực tiếp
sang phía Example Corp truy cập tự do, làm mờ ranh giới ownership — không đảm bảo chặt chẽ nguyên tắc "customer retains full ownership and control" như cơ chế data accessor có kiểm soát của Q Business. Kendra cũng không có khái niệm "data accessor" managed sẵn cho mô hình chia sẻ có kiểm soát này.
🔑

(x-amz-meta-*)

=== Question 37 ===
A company is building a generative AI (GenAI) application that uses Amazon Bedrock APIs to process complex customer inquiries. During peak usage periods, the application experiences intermittent API timeouts that cause issues such as broken response chunks and delayed data delivery. The application struggles to ensure that prompts remain within token limits when handling complex customer inquiries of varying lengths. Users have reported truncated inputs and incomplete responses. The company has also observed foundation model (FM) invocation failures.
The company needs a retry strategy that automatically handles transient service errors and prevents overwhelming Amazon Bedrock during peak usage periods. The strategy must also adapt to changing service availability and support response streaming and token-aware request handling.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Use the AWS SDK to configure a retry strategy in standard mode. Wrap Amazon Bedrock API calls in try-catch blocks that handle timeout exceptions. Return cached completions for failed streaming requests. Enforce a global token limit for all users. Add jitter-based retry logic and lightweight token trimming for each request. Resume broken streams by requesting only the missing chunks from the point of failure. Maintain a small in-memory buffer of the most recent chunks to minimize redundant data transfer.
B. Set Amazon Bedrock client request timeouts to 30 seconds. Implement client-side load shedding. Buffer partial results and stop new requests when the application performance begins to degrade. Set static token usage caps for all requests. Configure exponential backoff retries, dynamic chunk sizing, and context-aware token limits.
C. Implement an adaptive retry strategy that uses exponential backoff with jitter and a circuit breaker pattern that temporarily disables retries when error rates exceed a predefined threshold. Implement a streaming response handler that monitors for chunk delivery timeouts. Configure the handler to buffer successfully received chunks and intelligently resume streaming from the last received chunk when connections are re-established. (CORRECT)
D. Implement a standard retry strategy that uses a 1-second fixed delay between attempts and a 3-retry maximum for all errors. Handle streaming response timeouts by restarting streams. Cap token usage for each session.

Correct answer(s): C

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
GenAI app
dùng Bedrock, xử lý
complex customer inquiries
Peak usage
: intermittent API timeouts →
broken response chunks, delayed delivery
Prompts cần
stay within token limits
cho varying-length inputs
Đề bài có
4 yêu cầu chính
:
API timeout / transient errors
Peak usage, tránh làm quá tải Bedrock
Streaming response bị đứt giữa chừng
Service availability thay đổi
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Implement an adaptive retry strategy that uses exponential backoff with jitter and a circuit breaker pattern that temporarily disables retries when error rates exceed a predefined threshold. Implement a streaming response handler that monitors for chunk delivery timeouts. Configure the handler to buffer successfully received chunks and intelligently resume streaming from the last received chunk when connections are re-established.
1. Pattern Exponential Backoff + Jitter
Thay vì retry cố định mỗi 1 giây:
Request → fail
 ↓
retry 1 → wait 1s
retry 2 → wait 2s
retry 3 → wait 4s
retry 4 → wait 8s
Thêm
jitter
để nhiều client không cùng retry tại một thời điểm:
Client A → retry sau 2.3s
Client B → retry sau 1.7s
Client C → retry sau 2.8s
Điều này đặc biệt quan trọng trong
peak usage
, giúp tránh tình trạng:
Bedrock overloaded
 ↓
1000 requests fail
 ↓
1000 requests retry cùng lúc
 ↓
Bedrock càng overloaded
 ↓
retry tiếp...
2. Pattern Circuit Breaker
Khi tỷ lệ lỗi vượt ngưỡng:
Normal
 ↓
Errors tăng
 ↓
Circuit OPEN
 ↓
Tạm thời không retry
 ↓
Giảm pressure lên Bedrock
 ↓
Service ổn định
 ↓
Circuit HALF-OPEN
 ↓
Test request
 ↓
OK → CLOSE
Nó giải quyết trực tiếp yêu cầu:
"prevents overwhelming Amazon Bedrock during peak usage periods"
3. Streaming response
Đề bài nói có: broken response chunks and delayed data delivery. Giải pháp đáp án
: 'buffer successfully received chunks'
Ví dụ Bedrock trả về:
Chunk 1 ✓
Chunk 2 ✓
Chunk 3 ✓
Chunk 4 ✓
Chunk 5 ✗ timeout
Application giữ lại:
Chunk 1
Chunk 2
Chunk 3
Chunk 4
Sau khi connection được thiết lập lại, application có cơ chế tiếp tục xử lý từ trạng thái gần nhất thay vì mất toàn bộ response.
Lưu ý:
Đây là logic application-level; không nên hiểu là Bedrock tự động cung cấp cơ chế "resume từ chunk N" cho mọi streaming API. Trong thực tế, việc resume cần được thiết kế dựa trên API/protocol và khả năng idempotency của application.
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Implement a standard retry strategy that uses a 1-second fixed delay between attempts and a 3-retry maximum for all errors. Handle streaming response timeouts by restarting streams. Cap token usage for each session.
Fixed delay
→ thundering herd problem
Restart entire stream
→ inefficient, không resume từ last chunk
Session token cap
= blunt instrument, không token-aware
❌ Use the AWS SDK to configure a retry strategy in standard mode. Wrap Amazon Bedrock API calls in try-catch blocks that handle timeout exceptions. Return cached completions for failed streaming requests. Enforce a global token limit for all users. Add jitter-based retry logic and lightweight token trimming for each request. Resume broken streams by requesting only the missing chunks from the point of failure. Maintain a small in-memory buffer of the most recent chunks to minimize redundant data transfer.
Cached completions
cho streaming = không phù hợp (responses là dynamic)
Global token limit
= không per-request token-aware
"Requesting only missing chunks" = không phải cách streaming works trong Bedrock
❌ Set Amazon Bedrock client request timeouts to 30 seconds. Implement client-side load shedding. Buffer partial results and stop new requests when the application performance begins to degrade. Set static token usage caps for all requests. Configure exponential backoff retries, dynamic chunk sizing, and context-aware token limits.
Static token caps
→ không adaptive
Load shedding
= drop requests, không phải retry intelligently
Quá nhiều custom mechanisms → complex
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Prevent overwhelming service (thundering herd)
→
Exponential backoff with jitter
Adapt to changing availability
→
Circuit breaker pattern
(open when error rate high)
Broken streaming chunks
→
Buffer chunks + resume from last received chunk
Fixed delay retry
→ thundering herd → KHÔNG dùng
Circuit breaker
= temporarily disable retries (khác với retry logic thông thường)

============================================================

=== Question 38 ===
A company is implementing a serverless inference API by using AWS Lambda. The API will dynamically invoke multiple AI models hosted on Amazon Bedrock. The company needs to design a solution that can switch between model providers without modifying or redeploying Lambda code in real time. The design must include safe rollout of configuration changes and validation and rollback capabilities.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Store the active model provider in AWS Systems Manager Parameter Store. Configure a Lambda function to read the parameter at runtime to determine which model to invoke.
B. Store the active model provider in AWS AppConfig. Configure a Lambda function to read the configuration at runtime to determine which model to invoke. (CORRECT)
C. Store the active model provider in a JSON file hosted on Amazon S3. Use AWS AppConfig to reference the S3 file as a hosted configuration source. Configure a Lambda function to read the file through AppConfig at runtime to determine which model to invoke.
D. Configure an Amazon API Gateway REST API to route requests to separate Lambda functions. Hardcode each Lambda function to a specific model provider. Switch the integration target manually.

Correct answer(s): B

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
Serverless inference API
dùng
AWS Lambda
, invoke multiple AI models trên
Amazon Bedrock
Cần
switch between model providers
mà
KHÔNG modify hoặc redeploy Lambda code
Yêu cầu:
safe rollout
,
validation
, và
rollback capabilities
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Store the active model provider in AWS AppConfig. Configure a Lambda function to read the configuration at runtime to determine which model to invoke.
AWS AppConfig
= configuration management với:
Safe rollout
(deployment strategies: linear, exponential)
Validation
(validators before deployment)
Rollback capabilities
(automatic rollback nếu CloudWatch alarms trigger)
Lambda reads config at runtime → switch models
without code changes
AppConfig hỗ trợ feature flags và dynamic configuration → perfect fit
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Store the active model provider in AWS Systems Manager Parameter Store. Configure a Lambda function to read the parameter at runtime to determine which model to invoke.
Parameter Store = simple config storage, KHÔNG có:
Safe rollout strategies
Built-in validation
Automatic rollback
Chỉ là key-value store, không có deployment management
❌ Configure an Amazon API Gateway REST API to route requests to separate Lambda functions. Hardcode each Lambda function to a specific model provider. Switch the integration target manually.
Hardcode
= vi phạm yêu cầu "không modify Lambda code"
Manual switch
= không phải safe rollout/automated
❌ Store the active model provider in a JSON file hosted on Amazon S3. Use AWS AppConfig to reference the S3 file as a hosted configuration source. Configure a Lambda function to read the file through AppConfig at runtime to determine which model to invoke.
Gần đúng nhưng phức tạp không cần thiết: S3 + AppConfig reference
AppConfig có thể store configuration trực tiếp mà không cần S3 intermediary
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Safe rollout + validation + rollback + no code change
→
AWS AppConfig
AppConfig vs Parameter Store
: AppConfig = deployment management | Parameter Store = simple config storage
AppConfig features
: deployment strategies, validators, automatic rollback → exact fit cho "safe rollout"
Hardcode trong Lambda
→ phải redeploy → vi phạm requirement
Khi thấy "switch without redeploy + safe rollout + rollback" →
AppConfig
luôn

=== Question 39 ===
A hotel company wants to enhance a legacy Java-based property management system (PMS) by adding AI capabilities. The solution must maintain separate access controls for each hotel that the company manages. The solution must provide room availability information in near real time and must maintain consistent performance during peak usage periods.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Deploy a single Amazon Bedrock knowledge base that contains combined data for all hotels. Configure AWS Lambda functions to synchronize data from each hotel's PMS database through direct API connections. Implement AWS CloudTrail logging with hotel-specific filters to audit access logs for each hotel's data.
B. Build a centralized Amazon Bedrock agent that uses multiple knowledge bases. Implement AWS IAM Identity Center with hotel-specific permission sets to control hotel staff data access.
C. Create an Amazon EventBridge rule for each hotel that is invoked by changes to the PMS database for each hotel. Configure the rule to send updates to a centralized Amazon Bedrock knowledge base in a management AWS account. Configure resource-based policies to enforce hotel-specific access controls for hotel staff.
D. Implement one Amazon Bedrock knowledge base for each hotel in a multi-account structure. Use direct data ingestion to provide real-time room availability information. Schedule regular synchronization for less critical information. (CORRECT)

Correct answer(s): D

Explanation:
📝

Hotel company
, legacy
Java-based PMS
, thêm AI capabilities
Dùng
Amazon Bedrock Knowledge Bases
cho room availability + hotel-specific details
Yêu cầu:
Separate access controls cho mỗi hotel
Near real-time
room availability information
Consistent performance during peak usage
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Implement one Amazon Bedrock knowledge base for each hotel in a multi-account structure. Use direct data ingestion to provide real-time room availability information. Schedule regular synchronization for less critical information.
One KB per hotel
→ separate access controls tự nhiên (per-KB IAM policies)
Multi-account structure
→ strongest isolation giữa hotels
Direct data ingestion
→ near real-time updates cho room availability
Scheduled sync
cho less critical data → balance performance và cost
Consistent performance: dedicated KB per hotel → không chia sẻ resources
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Deploy a single Amazon Bedrock knowledge base that contains combined data for all hotels. Configure AWS Lambda functions to synchronize data from each hotel's PMS database through direct API connections. Implement AWS CloudTrail logging with hotel-specific filters to audit access logs for each hotel's data.
Single KB cho tất cả hotels
→ khó enforce separate access controls
CloudTrail filters ≠ proper access controls
Single KB = potential data leakage giữa hotels
❌ Create an Amazon EventBridge rule for each hotel that is invoked by changes to the PMS database for each hotel. Configure the rule to send updates to a centralized Amazon Bedrock knowledge base in a management AWS account. Configure resource-based policies to enforce hotel-specific access controls for hotel staff.
Centralized KB
→ tất cả hotels share một KB → weaker isolation
Resource-based policies = có thể complex để manage per-hotel
EventBridge → Lambda → KB = near real-time nhưng thêm complexity
❌ Build a centralized Amazon Bedrock agent that uses multiple knowledge bases. Implement AWS IAM Identity Center with hotel-specific permission sets to control hotel staff data access.

IAM Identity Center = identity federation, phù hợp cho user auth nhưng không phải KB isolation

🔑
Tips and tricks:
Separate access controls + isolation per entity
→
Separate KB per entity + multi-account
Near real-time data
→
Direct data ingestion
vào KB (không phải scheduled only)
Consistent performance

Multi-account
→ strongest isolation boundary trong AWS
Single KB tất cả
→ data isolation risk → KHÔNG phù hợp khi cần separate access controls

=== Question 40 ===

Create a pipeline in AWS CodePipeline pipeline that has a deployment stage for each environment that uses AWS CodeBuild deploy actions. (CORRECT)

Configure the pipeline to deploy to the production environment by using an AWS CodeBuild deploy action. For the development environment, manually recreate the resources by referring to the production application code.
D. Create a separate AWS CDK application for each environment. Configure the applications to invoke the Amazon Bedrock FMs by using the aws_bedrock.FoundationModel.fromFoundationModelId() method. Create a separate pipeline in AWS CodePipeline for each environment.

easily switch between FMs
for testing
✅

Các đáp án sai:
❌ Create one AWS CDK application. Create multiple pipelines in AWS CodePipeline. Configure each pipeline to have its own settings for each FM. Configure the application to invoke the Amazon Bedrock FMs by using the aws_bedrock.ProvisionedModel.fromProvisionedModelArn() method.

= not automated, error-prone

=== Question 41 ===
A healthcare company is using Amazon Bedrock to develop a real-time patient care AI assistant to respond to queries for separate departments that handle clinical inquiries, insurance verification, appointment scheduling, and insurance claims. The company wants to use a multi-agent architecture.
The company must ensure that the AI assistant is scalable and can onboard new features for patients. The AI assistant must be able to handle thousands of parallel patient interactions. The company must ensure that patients receive appropriate domain-specific responses to queries.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Isolate data for each agent by using separate knowledge bases. Use IAM filtering to control access to each knowledge base. Deploy a supervisor agent to perform natural language intent classification on patient inquiries. Configure the supervisor agent to route queries to specialized collaborator agents to respond to department-specific queries. Configure each specialized collaborator agent to use Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) with the agent's department-specific knowledge base. (CORRECT)
B. Create a separate supervisor agent for each department. Configure individual collaborator agents to perform natural language intent classification for each specialty domain within each department. Integrate each collaborator agent with department-specific knowledge bases only. Implement manual handoff processes between the supervisor agents.
C. Isolate data for each department in separate knowledge bases. Use IAM filtering to control access to each knowledge base. Deploy a single general-purpose agent. Configure multiple action groups within the general-purpose agent to perform specific department functions. Implement rule-based routing logic within the general-purpose agent instructions.
D. Implement multiple independent supervisor agents that run in parallel to respond to patient inquiries for each department. Configure multiple collaborator agents for each supervisor agent. Integrate all agents with the same knowledge base. Use external routing logic to merge responses from multiple supervisor agents.

Correct answer(s): A

Explanation:

Healthcare company
, Amazon Bedrock,
multi-agent architecture

Scalable
, onboard new features easily
Handle
thousands of parallel patient interactions
Domain-specific responses
cho từng department
✅
Đáp án đúng:

Deploy a supervisor agent to perform natural language intent classification on patient inquiries. Configure the supervisor agent to route queries to specialized collaborator agents to respond to department-specific queries. Configure each specialized collaborator agent to use Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) with the agent's department-specific knowledge base.
Supervisor + collaborator agents
= đúng multi-agent architecture trong Amazon Bedrock
Supervisor
classify intent → route đến đúng department agent
Separate KBs per department
→ domain-specific responses + data isolation
IAM filtering
→ access control per KB
Scalable
: thêm department = thêm collaborator agent mới dễ dàng
Bedrock native multi-agent support → handle parallel interactions
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Create a separate supervisor agent for each department. Configure individual collaborator agents to perform natural language intent classification for each specialty domain within each department. Integrate each collaborator agent with department-specific knowledge bases only. Implement manual handoff processes between the supervisor agents.
Multiple supervisors + manual handoff
= NOT scalable, không handle parallel interactions tốt
Manual handoff = latency, không automated
❌ Isolate data for each department in separate knowledge bases. Use IAM filtering to control access to each knowledge base. Deploy a single general-purpose agent. Configure multiple action groups within the general-purpose agent to perform specific department functions. Implement rule-based routing logic within the general-purpose agent instructions.

Integrate all agents with the same knowledge base. Use external routing logic to merge responses from multiple supervisor agents.
Same knowledge base
→ không phân biệt domain-specific knowledge
External routing logic = custom complexity, not native Bedrock
Merge responses từ multiple supervisors = không phù hợp
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Multi-agent + domain-specific routing
→
Supervisor agent + Collaborator agents
(Bedrock native)
Scalable multi-agent
→ Supervisor classify intent → route → Collaborators (thêm collaborator = scale)
Separate data per agent
→
Separate Knowledge Bases + IAM filtering
Rule-based routing
→ brittle, NOT multi-agent pattern
Bedrock Multi-Agent pattern
: 1 Supervisor orchestrates N Collaborators, mỗi Collaborator có KB riêng

=== Question 43 ===
A company is creating a workflow to review customer-facing communications before the company sends the communications. The company uses a pre-defined message template to generate the communications and stores the communications in an Amazon S3 bucket. The workflow needs to capture a specific portion from the template and send it to an Amazon Bedrock model. The workflow must store model responses back to the original S3 bucket.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Create an Amazon Bedrock agent that has an action group. Configure instructions to define how the agent should parse the communications. Configure the action group to retrieve the communications from the S3 bucket, invoke the Amazon Bedrock model, and store the model responses back to the S3 bucket.
B. Create an Amazon Bedrock agent that has a single action group. Configure three AWS Lambda functions in the action group. Configure the functions to retrieve the communications from the S3 bucket, parse the communications and invoke the Amazon Bedrock model, and store the model responses back to the S3 bucket.
C. Create an AWS Step Functions Express workflow state machine. Use an Amazon S3 integration GetObject step to retrieve the original communications. Use an intrinsic function Pass step to parse the communications and to pass the results to an Amazon Bedrock InvokeModel step. Configure an Amazon S3 integration PutObject step to store the model responses back to the S3 bucket.
D. Create a flow in Amazon Bedrock Flows. Configure S3 action nodes at the beginning and end of the flow to retrieve and store the communications and the model responses. In the middle of the flow, configure an expression to parse each communication. Configure an agent step to send the parsed input to the model for review. (CORRECT)

Correct answer(s): D

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
Company review
customer-facing communications
trước khi gửi
Dùng
pre-defined message template
lưu trong
S3
Workflow cần:
Capture specific portion
từ template
Send to Bedrock model
để review
Store model responses back
to original S3 bucket
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Create a flow in Amazon Bedrock Flows. Configure S3 action nodes at the beginning and end of the flow to retrieve and store the communications and the model responses. In the middle of the flow, configure an expression to parse each communication. Configure an agent step to send the parsed input to the model for review.
Amazon Bedrock Flows
= visual workflow orchestration native trong Bedrock
S3 action nodes
→ native S3 read/write operations trong Flows
Expression node
→ parse/extract specific portion từ template
Agent step
→ invoke Bedrock model cho review
Toàn bộ native Bedrock Flows → LEAST operational overhead
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Create an AWS Step Functions Express workflow state machine. Use an Amazon S3 integration GetObject step to retrieve the original communications. Use an intrinsic function Pass step to parse the communications and to pass the results to an Amazon Bedrock InvokeModel step. Configure an Amazon S3 integration PutObject step to store the model responses back to the S3 bucket.
Step Functions approach phức tạp hơn Bedrock Flows cho use case đơn giản này
Step Functions Express = short-lived (max 5 min) nhưng vẫn thêm operational overhead
Bedrock Flows được designed specifically cho use case này
❌ Create an Amazon Bedrock agent that has an action group. Configure instructions to define how the agent should parse the communications. Configure the action group to retrieve the communications from the S3 bucket, invoke the Amazon Bedrock model, and store the model responses back to the S3 bucket.
Bedrock Agent = conversational agent với memory/reasoning, overkill cho simple workflow
Agent không phải optimal cho deterministic sequential processing workflow
❌ Create an Amazon Bedrock agent that has a single action group. Configure three AWS Lambda functions in the action group. Configure the functions to retrieve the communications from the S3 bucket, parse the communications and invoke the Amazon Bedrock model, and store the model responses back to the S3 bucket.
3 separate Lambda functions = unnecessary complexity
Lambda functions thêm operational overhead
Bedrock Flows với native nodes là simpler và more appropriate
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Sequential workflow: S3 read → process → Bedrock invoke → S3 write
→
Amazon Bedrock Flows
Bedrock Flows nodes
: S3 node, Expression node, Agent/Model node → deterministic workflow
Bedrock Flows
vs
Bedrock Agent
: Flows = deterministic pipeline | Agent = conversational/reasoning
Step Functions
= workflow orchestration nhưng Bedrock Flows is simpler cho Bedrock-centric workflows
S3 action nodes
trong Bedrock Flows = native S3 integration (không cần Lambda)

=== Question 44 ===
A company wants to create an annual rewards program for its customers. The rewards that customers earn vary based on different parameters such as the categories of the items ordered and the customers' purchase history.
The company needs a generative AI (GenAI) solution that uses three Amazon Bedrock agents to help customers during online catalog browsing. The agents must use knowledge bases and action groups to handle the search, recommendation, and order modules. The modules must operate sequentially. An AWS Lambda function must calculate estimated rewards for each recommended item. The solution must provide graceful degradation during service disruptions.
Which solution will meet these requirements with the MOST operational efficiency?

A. Define an Amazon API Gateway REST API behind each agent. Create a second Lambda function to orchestrate the calls to the agents and the rewards Lambda function. Configure the second Lambda function with a retry/fallback mechanism.
B. Create an AWS Step Functions state machine with four tasks that run the agents and the rewards Lambda function. Set up retry and catch branches for each of the task steps. (CORRECT)
C. Configure each agent with a separate retry/fallback mechanism. Create a second Lambda function to orchestrate the calls to the agents and the rewards Lambda function. Define an Amazon API Gateway REST API behind the second Lambda function.
D. Create a second Lambda function to orchestrate the calls to the agents and the rewards Lambda function. Create an AWS Step Functions state machine with one task that runs the second Lambda function. Set up retry and catch branches for the task step.

Correct answer(s): B

Explanation:
📝
Tóm tắt đề:
Annual rewards program
, 3 Bedrock agents (search, recommendation, order modules)
Agents dùng
knowledge bases + action groups
Modules chạy
sequentially
Lambda function
tính toán estimated rewards
Cần
graceful degradation
khi service disruptions
MOST operational efficiency
✅
Đáp án đúng:
Create an AWS Step Functions state machine with four tasks that run the agents and the rewards Lambda function. Set up retry and catch branches for each of the task steps.
Step Functions state machine
→ orchestrate sequential agents (search → recommendation → order → Lambda rewards)
4 tasks
= 3 agents + 1 Lambda function = exactly matching requirements
Retry và catch branches PER TASK
→ graceful degradation at each step (nếu một bước fail, có thể catch và handle)
Step Functions
= optimal cho sequential workflow orchestration
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Define an Amazon API Gateway REST API behind each agent. Create a second Lambda function to orchestrate the calls to the agents and the rewards Lambda function. Configure the second Lambda function with a retry/fallback mechanism.
API Gateway behind each agent = unnecessary complexity
Custom orchestration Lambda = MORE operational overhead vs Step Functions
Retry/fallback trong Lambda code = fragile
❌ Configure each agent with a separate retry/fallback mechanism. Create a second Lambda function to orchestrate the calls to the agents and the rewards Lambda function. Define an Amazon API Gateway REST API behind the second Lambda function.
Retry per agent
+ orchestration Lambda = redundant layers
NOT MOST operational efficiency
❌ Create a second Lambda function to orchestrate the calls to the agents and the rewards Lambda function. Create an AWS Step Functions state machine with one task that runs the second Lambda function. Set up retry and catch branches for the task step.
One task
wrap everything trong Lambda = không granular retry/catch per step
Nếu Lambda fails, không biết step nào failed → kém graceful degradation
🔑
Tips and tricks:
Sequential agents + graceful degradation
→
Step Functions state machine
với
retry và catch per task
MOST operational efficiency
→ Step Functions managed orchestration (không phải custom Lambda orchestration)
Graceful degradation
→ catch branches trong Step Functions (khi một bước fail → fallback path)
4 tasks trong Step Functions
= 3 agents + 1 Lambda = exact mapping
API Gateway behind each agent
= overkill complexity, không operational efficient

=== Question 45 ===
A company uses AWS Lambda functions to build an AI agent solution. A GenAI developer must set up a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server that accesses user information. The GenAI developer must also configure the AI agent to use the new MCP server. The GenAI developer must ensure that only authorized users can access the MCP server.
Which solution will meet these requirements?

A. Use a Lambda function to host the MCP server. Grant the AI agent Lambda functions permission to invoke the Lambda function that hosts the MCP server. Configure the AI agent's MCP client to invoke the MCP server asynchronously.
B. Use a Lambda function to host the MCP server. Grant the AI agent Lambda functions permission to invoke the Lambda function that hosts the MCP server. Configure the AI agent to use the STDIO transport with the MCP server.
C. Use a Lambda function to host the MCP server. Create an Amazon API Gateway HTTP API that proxies requests to the Lambda function. Configure the AI agent solution to use the Streamable HTTP transport to make requests through the HTTP API. Use Amazon Cognito to enforce OAuth 2.1. (CORRECT)
D. Use a Lambda layer to host the MCP server. Add the Lambda layer to the AI agent Lambda functions. Configure the agentic AI solution to use the STDIO transport to send requests to the MCP server. In the AI agent's MCP configuration, specify the Lambda layer ARN as the command. Specify the user credentials as environment variables.

Model Context Protocol (MCP) server

mới được access MCP server
✅

Use a Lambda function to host the MCP server. Create an Amazon API Gateway HTTP API that proxies requests to the Lambda function. Configure the AI agent solution to use the Streamable HTTP transport to make requests through the HTTP API. Use Amazon Cognito to enforce OAuth 2.1.
Lambda
host MCP server = serverless, scalable
API Gateway HTTP API
= expose MCP server securely
Streamable HTTP transport
= MCP standard transport cho remote servers (HTTP-based)
Amazon Cognito + OAuth 2.1
= proper authorization mechanism cho MCP (MCP spec yêu cầu OAuth 2.1 cho remote servers)
Đáp ứng "only authorized users" requirement
Các đáp án sai:
❌ Use a Lambda function to host the MCP server. Grant the AI agent Lambda functions permission to invoke the Lambda function that hosts the MCP server. Configure the AI agent's MCP client to invoke the MCP server asynchronously.
Asynchronous invocation
= không phù hợp cho MCP (cần synchronous request-response)
Không có authorization mechanism
❌ Use a Lambda function to host the MCP server. Grant the AI agent Lambda functions permission to invoke the Lambda function that hosts the MCP server. Configure the AI agent to use the STDIO transport with the MCP server.
STDIO transport
= chỉ dùng cho
local
MCP servers (same process/machine)
Lambda-hosted MCP server là
remote
→ cần
HTTP transport
(không phải STDIO)

In the AI agent's MCP configuration, specify the Lambda layer ARN as the command. Specify the user credentials as environment variables.

không phải cách host MCP server (layer = code/library sharing)
STDIO transport
= local only, không phù hợp
Credentials trong environment variables
= insecure, không phải proper auth
🔑

Remote MCP server
→
Streamable HTTP transport
(không phải STDIO)
STDIO transport
→ chỉ cho
local/same-process
MCP servers
MCP authorization
→
OAuth 2.1
(MCP specification requirement cho remote servers)
Amazon Cognito
= OAuth 2.1 provider trong AWS ecosystem
Lambda layer
= shared code/dependencies, KHÔNG phải server hosting
